          <li>
            The TRV of <emu-grammar><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-HexDigit">HexDigit</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>::</emu-geq> <emu-oneof>one of</emu-oneof> <emu-t>0</emu-t> <emu-t>1</emu-t> <emu-t>2</emu-t> <emu-t>3</emu-t> <emu-t>4</emu-t> <emu-t>5</emu-t> <emu-t>6</emu-t> <emu-t>7</emu-t> <emu-t>8</emu-t> <emu-t>9</emu-t> <emu-t>a</emu-t> <emu-t>b</emu-t> <emu-t>c</emu-t> <emu-t>d</emu-t> <emu-t>e</emu-t> <emu-t>f</emu-t> <emu-t>A</emu-t> <emu-t>B</emu-t> <emu-t>C</emu-t> <emu-t>D</emu-t> <emu-t>E</emu-t> <emu-t>F</emu-t></emu-grammar> is the result of performing <emu-xref aoid="UTF16EncodeCodePoint"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-utf16encodecodepoint">UTF16EncodeCodePoint</a></emu-xref> on the single code point matched by this production.
          </li>
          <li>
            The TRV of <emu-grammar><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-LineContinuation">LineContinuation</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>::</emu-geq> <emu-t>\</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-LineTerminatorSequence">LineTerminatorSequence</a></emu-nt></emu-grammar> is the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#string-concatenation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#string-concatenation">string-concatenation</a></emu-xref> of the code unit 0x005C (REVERSE SOLIDUS) and the TRV of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-LineTerminatorSequence">LineTerminatorSequence</a></emu-nt>.
          </li>
          <li>
            The TRV of <emu-grammar><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-LineTerminatorSequence">LineTerminatorSequence</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>::</emu-geq> &lt;<emu-nt>LF</emu-nt>&gt;</emu-grammar> is the String value consisting of the code unit 0x000A (LINE FEED).
          </li>
          <li>
            The TRV of <emu-grammar><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-LineTerminatorSequence">LineTerminatorSequence</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>::</emu-geq> &lt;<emu-nt>CR</emu-nt>&gt;</emu-grammar> is the String value consisting of the code unit 0x000A (LINE FEED).
          </li>
          <li>
            The TRV of <emu-grammar><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-LineTerminatorSequence">LineTerminatorSequence</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>::</emu-geq> &lt;<emu-nt>LS</emu-nt>&gt;</emu-grammar> is the String value consisting of the code unit 0x2028 (LINE SEPARATOR).
          </li>
          <li>
            The TRV of <emu-grammar><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-LineTerminatorSequence">LineTerminatorSequence</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>::</emu-geq> &lt;<emu-nt>PS</emu-nt>&gt;</emu-grammar> is the String value consisting of the code unit 0x2029 (PARAGRAPH SEPARATOR).
          </li>
          <li>
            The TRV of <emu-grammar><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-LineTerminatorSequence">LineTerminatorSequence</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>::</emu-geq> &lt;<emu-nt>CR</emu-nt>&gt; &lt;<emu-nt>LF</emu-nt>&gt;</emu-grammar> is the String value consisting of the code unit 0x000A (LINE FEED).
          </li>
        </ul>
        <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p><emu-xref aoid="TV"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-static-semantics-tv">TV</a></emu-xref> excludes the code units of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-LineContinuation">LineContinuation</a></emu-nt> while TRV includes them. &lt;CR>&lt;LF> and &lt;CR> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-LineTerminatorSequence">LineTerminatorSequence</a></emu-nt>s are normalized to &lt;LF> for both <emu-xref aoid="TV"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-static-semantics-tv">TV</a></emu-xref> and TRV. An explicit <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-TemplateEscapeSequence">TemplateEscapeSequence</a></emu-nt> is needed to include a &lt;CR> or &lt;CR>&lt;LF> sequence.</p>
        </div></emu-note>
